class=\"col-span-1 p-3\"><span><svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" fill=\"none\" role=\"presentation\" class=\"text-fgAccentPrimary1 inline size-6\"><path d=\"M6.75 13.0625L9.9 16.25L17.25 7.75\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"1.5\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\"></path></svg></span></div></div></div></details></div></section></div></div><div class=\"px-6\"><section id=\"faqs\" class=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-16 lg:space-y-32\"><div class=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 pt-16 lg:grid-cols-3 lg:pt-32\"><div class=\"lg:text-left\"><div class=\"space-y-2 text-fg0\"><div class=\"flex flex-col gap-4\"><h2 class=\"z-20 text-pretty tracking-tight lg:text-4xl text-3xl font-light\"><span>FAQs<!-- --> </span></h2></div></div></div><div class=\"-mx-6 w-screen md:mx-auto md:w-full lg:col-span-2\"><details class=\"group w-full last:border-b-0\"><summary class=\"flex cursor-pointer list-none items-start justify-between gap-4 py-6 pr-3 pl-6 border-separator1 text-fg1 border-t text-lg text-pretty group-last:border-b group-open:border-b hover:bg-bg2 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:bg-bg2 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 group-open:bg-bg2 transition-colors\"><div class=\"flex-1 self-center\">What's the difference between agent deployments, concurrent agent sessions, and LiveKit Inference concurrency?</div><svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" width=\"32\" height=\"32\" fill=\"none\" role=\"presentation\" class=\"size-8 transition-transform duration-200 ease-out group-open:rotate-180\"><path d=\"M8 10L12 14L16 10\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"1.5\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\"></path></svg></summary><div class=\"p-6\"><div class=\"text-fg2 space-y-4\"><p>An <strong>agent deployment</strong> is a running version of your agent backend hosted on LiveKit Cloud, typically with a unique prompt, set of voice AI models, and function calls. You can configure your agent to complete different<!-- --> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"text-pretty decoration-1 underline-offset-[0.15em] transition-colors text-fg0 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 underline\" href=\"https://docs.livekit.io/agents/logic/tasks/\">tasks</a> <!-- -->or<!-- --> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"text-pretty decoration-1 underline-offset-[0.15em] transition-colors text-fg0 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 underline\" href=\"https://docs.livekit.io/agents/logic/workflows/\">workflows</a>. Deploy separate agents when you need distinct reasoning behavior or tool access (e.g., a front-office receptionist agent to handle inbound phone calls for appointment scheduling and triage vs a back-office agent to make outbound calls to insurance providers to verify patient coverage).</p><p>A <strong>concurrent agent session</strong> is a live interaction between your agent and an end user. If your agent is handling 10 calls or conversations at the same time, that counts as 10 concurrent sessions, regardless of how many agent deployments you have on LiveKit Cloud.</p><p><strong>LiveKit Inference concurrency</strong> refers specifically to how many AI inference requests across LLM, STT, and TTS can run at the same time through LiveKit Inference. It limits how many model calls can be processed concurrently, independent of how many agent sessions or deployments you have. The LiveKit Inference concurrency limit for each plan applies to your aggregate usage of a model type (e.g., total connections to any LiveKit Inference STT). For example, if there are 10 concurrent agent sessions running and the agent is configured to use LiveKit Inference for STT, then there are 10 concurrent STT connections.</p><p>For more information on LiveKit Cloud quotas and limits, refer to our<!-- --> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"text-pretty decoration-1 underline-offset-[0.15em] transition-colors text-fg0 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 underline\" href=\"https://docs.livekit.io/deploy/admin/quotas-and-limits/\">docs</a>.</p></div></div></details><details class=\"group w-full last:border-b-0\"><summary class=\"flex cursor-pointer list-none items-start justify-between gap-4 py-6 pr-3 pl-6 border-separator1 text-fg1 border-t text-lg text-pretty group-last:border-b group-open:border-b hover:bg-bg2 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:bg-bg2 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 group-open:bg-bg2 transition-colors\"><div class=\"flex-1 self-center\">Can I self-host LiveKit?</div><svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" width=\"32\" height=\"32\" fill=\"none\" role=\"presentation\" class=\"size-8 transition-transform duration-200 ease-out group-open:rotate-180\"><path d=\"M8 10L12 14L16 10\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"1.5\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\"></path></svg></summary><div class=\"p-6\"><div class=\"text-fg2 space-y-4\"><p>The LiveKit Agents framework and LiveKit media server are both completely open source and available to run locally or host on your own infrastructure.</p><p>LiveKit Cloud is the best way to run LiveKit in production, with fully managed agent deployments, built-in observability and dashboards, and ultra low-latency global media transport.</p><p><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"text-pretty decoration-1 underline-offset-[0.15em] transition-colors text-fg0 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 underline\" href=\"https://cloud.livekit.io/\">Sign up for LiveKit Cloud here</a>, or refer to our docs on how to<!-- --> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"text-pretty decoration-1 underline-offset-[0.15em] transition-colors text-fg0 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 underline\" href=\"https://docs.livekit.io/home/self-hosting/local/\">run LiveKit's media server locally</a> <!-- -->or<!-- --> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" class=\"text-pretty decoration-1 underline-offset-[0.15em] transition-colors text-fg0 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 underline\" href=\"https://docs.livekit.io/agents/ops/deployment/custom/\">deploy LiveKit Agents in a custom environment</a>.</p></div></div></details><details class=\"group w-full last:border-b-0\"><summary class=\"flex cursor-pointer list-none items-start justify-between gap-4 py-6 pr-3 pl-6 border-separator1 text-fg1 border-t text-lg text-pretty group-last:border-b group-open:border-b hover:bg-bg2 hover:text-fgAccentPrimary1 focus:bg-bg2 focus:text-fgAccentPrimary1 group-open:bg-bg2 transition-colors\"><div class=\"flex-1 self-center\">Do you offer on-premise or private deployments?</div><svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" width=\"32\" height=\"32\" fill=\"none\" role=\"presentation\" class=\"size-8 transition-transform duration-200 ease-out group-open:rotate-180\"><path d=\"M8 10L12 14L16 10\"
